\doxysubsubsubsubsubsection{Descripción detallada}
UART Transmit and Receive functions. 

\begin{DoxyVerb} ===============================================================================
                      ##### IO operation functions #####
 ===============================================================================
    This subsection provides a set of functions allowing to manage the UART asynchronous
    and Half duplex data transfers.

    (#) There are two modes of transfer:
       (+) Blocking mode: The communication is performed in polling mode.
           The HAL status of all data processing is returned by the same function
           after finishing transfer.
       (+) Non-Blocking mode: The communication is performed using Interrupts
           or DMA, these API's return the HAL status.
           The end of the data processing will be indicated through the
           dedicated UART IRQ when using Interrupt mode or the DMA IRQ when
           using DMA mode.
           The HAL_UART_TxCpltCallback(), HAL_UART_RxCpltCallback() user callbacks
           will be executed respectively at the end of the transmit or receive process
           The HAL_UART_ErrorCallback()user callback will be executed when a communication error is detected.

    (#) Blocking mode API's are :
        (+) HAL_UART_Transmit()
        (+) HAL_UART_Receive()

    (#) Non-Blocking mode API's with Interrupt are :
        (+) HAL_UART_Transmit_IT()
        (+) HAL_UART_Receive_IT()
        (+) HAL_UART_IRQHandler()

    (#) Non-Blocking mode API's with DMA are :
        (+) HAL_UART_Transmit_DMA()
        (+) HAL_UART_Receive_DMA()
        (+) HAL_UART_DMAPause()
        (+) HAL_UART_DMAResume()
        (+) HAL_UART_DMAStop()

    (#) A set of Transfer Complete Callbacks are provided in Non_Blocking mode:
        (+) HAL_UART_TxHalfCpltCallback()
        (+) HAL_UART_TxCpltCallback()
        (+) HAL_UART_RxHalfCpltCallback()
        (+) HAL_UART_RxCpltCallback()
        (+) HAL_UART_ErrorCallback()

    (#) Non-Blocking mode transfers could be aborted using Abort API's :
        (+) HAL_UART_Abort()
        (+) HAL_UART_AbortTransmit()
        (+) HAL_UART_AbortReceive()
        (+) HAL_UART_Abort_IT()
        (+) HAL_UART_AbortTransmit_IT()
        (+) HAL_UART_AbortReceive_IT()

    (#) For Abort services based on interrupts (HAL_UART_Abortxxx_IT), a set of Abort Complete Callbacks are provided:
        (+) HAL_UART_AbortCpltCallback()
        (+) HAL_UART_AbortTransmitCpltCallback()
        (+) HAL_UART_AbortReceiveCpltCallback()

    (#) A Rx Event Reception Callback (Rx event notification) is available for Non_Blocking modes of enhanced reception services:
        (+) HAL_UARTEx_RxEventCallback()

    (#) In Non-Blocking mode transfers, possible errors are split into 2 categories.
        Errors are handled as follows :
       (+) Error is considered as Recoverable and non blocking : Transfer could go till end, but error severity is
           to be evaluated by user : this concerns Frame Error, Parity Error or Noise Error in Interrupt mode reception .
           Received character is then retrieved and stored in Rx buffer, Error code is set to allow user to identify error type,
           and HAL_UART_ErrorCallback() user callback is executed. Transfer is kept ongoing on UART side.
           If user wants to abort it, Abort services should be called by user.
       (+) Error is considered as Blocking : Transfer could not be completed properly and is aborted.
           This concerns Overrun Error In Interrupt mode reception and all errors in DMA mode.
           Error code is set to allow user to identify error type, and HAL_UART_ErrorCallback() user callback is executed.

    -@- In the Half duplex communication, it is forbidden to run the transmit
        and receive process in parallel, the UART state HAL_UART_STATE_BUSY_TX_RX can't be useful.
\end{DoxyVerb}

{\ttfamily \+\#include "{}\doxymbox{\hyperlink{stm32f1xx__hal__uart_8h}{stm32f1xx\+\_\+hal\+\_\+uart.\+h}}"{}}



Abort ongoing transfers (blocking mode). 


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parámetros}
{\em huart} & UART handle. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
This procedure could be used for aborting any ongoing transfer started in Interrupt or DMA mode. This procedure performs following operations :
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Disable UART Interrupts (Tx and Rx)
\item Disable the DMA transfer in the peripheral register (if enabled)
\item Abort DMA transfer by calling HAL\+\_\+\+DMA\+\_\+\+Abort (in case of transfer in DMA mode)
\item Set handle State to READY 
\end{DoxyItemize}

This procedure is executed in blocking mode : when exiting function, Abort is considered as completed. 
\end{DoxyNote}

\doxyparagraph{\texorpdfstring{HAL\_UART\_Abort\_IT()}{HAL\_UART\_Abort\_IT()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{group___u_a_r_t___exported___functions___group2_ga3183626ee21f103cbcb50241eca50e4d} 
\doxymbox{\hyperlink{stm32f1xx__hal__def_8h_a63c0679d1cb8b8c684fbb0632743478f}{HAL\+\_\+\+Status\+Type\+Def}} HAL\+\_\+\+UART\+\_\+\+Abort\+\_\+\+IT (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{group___u_a_r_t___exported___types_ga5de4a49eb132735325e706f406c69d6e}{UART\+\_\+\+Handle\+Type\+Def}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{huart}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



{\ttfamily \+\#include "{}\doxymbox{\hyperlink{stm32f1xx__hal__uart_8h}{stm32f1xx\+\_\+hal\+\_\+uart.\+h}}"{}}



Abort ongoing transfers (Interrupt mode). 


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parámetros}
{\em huart} & UART handle. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
This procedure could be used for aborting any ongoing transfer started in Interrupt or DMA mode. This procedure performs following operations :
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Disable UART Interrupts (Tx and Rx)
\item Disable the DMA transfer in the peripheral register (if enabled)
\item Abort DMA transfer by calling HAL\+\_\+\+DMA\+\_\+\+Abort\+\_\+\+IT (in case of transfer in DMA mode)
\item Set handle State to READY
\item At abort completion, call user abort complete callback 
\end{DoxyItemize}

This procedure is executed in Interrupt mode, meaning that abort procedure could be considered as completed only when user abort complete callback is executed (not when exiting function). 
\end{DoxyNote}

{\ttfamily \+\#include "{}\doxymbox{\hyperlink{stm32f1xx__hal__uart_8h}{stm32f1xx\+\_\+hal\+\_\+uart.\+h}}"{}}



Abort ongoing Receive transfer (blocking mode). 


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parámetros}
{\em huart} & UART handle. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
This procedure could be used for aborting any ongoing Rx transfer started in Interrupt or DMA mode. This procedure performs following operations :
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Disable UART Interrupts (Rx)
\item Disable the DMA transfer in the peripheral register (if enabled)
\item Abort DMA transfer by calling HAL\+\_\+\+DMA\+\_\+\+Abort (in case of transfer in DMA mode)
\item Set handle State to READY 
\end{DoxyItemize}

This procedure is executed in blocking mode : when exiting function, Abort is considered as completed. 
\end{DoxyNote}

Abort ongoing Receive transfer (Interrupt mode). 


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parámetros}
{\em huart} & UART handle. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
This procedure could be used for aborting any ongoing Rx transfer started in Interrupt or DMA mode. This procedure performs following operations :
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Disable UART Interrupts (Rx)
\item Disable the DMA transfer in the peripheral register (if enabled)
\item Abort DMA transfer by calling HAL\+\_\+\+DMA\+\_\+\+Abort\+\_\+\+IT (in case of transfer in DMA mode)
\item Set handle State to READY
\item At abort completion, call user abort complete callback 
\end{DoxyItemize}

This procedure is executed in Interrupt mode, meaning that abort procedure could be considered as completed only when user abort complete callback is executed (not when exiting function). 
\end{DoxyNote}

Abort ongoing Transmit transfer (blocking mode). 


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parámetros}
{\em huart} & UART handle. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
This procedure could be used for aborting any ongoing Tx transfer started in Interrupt or DMA mode. This procedure performs following operations :
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Disable UART Interrupts (Tx)
\item Disable the DMA transfer in the peripheral register (if enabled)
\item Abort DMA transfer by calling HAL\+\_\+\+DMA\+\_\+\+Abort (in case of transfer in DMA mode)
\item Set handle State to READY 
\end{DoxyItemize}

This procedure is executed in blocking mode : when exiting function, Abort is considered as completed. 
\end{DoxyNote}

Abort ongoing Transmit transfer (Interrupt mode). 


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parámetros}
{\em huart} & UART handle. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
This procedure could be used for aborting any ongoing Tx transfer started in Interrupt or DMA mode. This procedure performs following operations :
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Disable UART Interrupts (Tx)
\item Disable the DMA transfer in the peripheral register (if enabled)
\item Abort DMA transfer by calling HAL\+\_\+\+DMA\+\_\+\+Abort\+\_\+\+IT (in case of transfer in DMA mode)
\item Set handle State to READY
\item At abort completion, call user abort complete callback 
\end{DoxyItemize}

This procedure is executed in Interrupt mode, meaning that abort procedure could be considered as completed only when user abort complete callback is executed (not when exiting function). 
\end{DoxyNote}

Receives an amount of data in blocking mode. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
When UART parity is not enabled (PCE = 0), and Word Length is configured to 9 bits (M1-\/\+M0 = 01), the received data is handled as a set of u16. In this case, Size must indicate the number of u16 available through p\+Data. 
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyParams}{Parámetros}
{\em huart} & Pointer to a \doxylink{group___u_a_r_t___exported___types_ga5de4a49eb132735325e706f406c69d6e}{UART\+\_\+\+Handle\+Type\+Def} structure that contains the configuration information for the specified UART module. \\
\hline
{\em p\+Data} & Pointer to data buffer (u8 or u16 data elements). \\
\hline
{\em Size} & Amount of data elements (u8 or u16) to be received. \\
\hline
{\em Timeout} & Timeout duration \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

Receives an amount of data in DMA mode. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
When UART parity is not enabled (PCE = 0), and Word Length is configured to 9 bits (M1-\/\+M0 = 01), the received data is handled as a set of u16. In this case, Size must indicate the number of u16 available through p\+Data. 
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyParams}{Parámetros}
{\em huart} & Pointer to a \doxylink{group___u_a_r_t___exported___types_ga5de4a49eb132735325e706f406c69d6e}{UART\+\_\+\+Handle\+Type\+Def} structure that contains the configuration information for the specified UART module. \\
\hline
{\em p\+Data} & Pointer to data buffer (u8 or u16 data elements). \\
\hline
{\em Size} & Amount of data elements (u8 or u16) to be received. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
When the UART parity is enabled (PCE = 1) the received data contains the parity bit. 
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Valores devueltos}
{\em \doxylink{group___h_a_l}{HAL}} & status \\
\hline
\end{DoxyRetVals}
\texorpdfstring{$<$}{<} Standard reception ~\newline

Sends an amount of data in blocking mode. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
When UART parity is not enabled (PCE = 0), and Word Length is configured to 9 bits (M1-\/\+M0 = 01), the sent data is handled as a set of u16. In this case, Size must indicate the number of u16 provided through p\+Data. 
\end{DoxyNote}

Sends an amount of data in DMA mode. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
When UART parity is not enabled (PCE = 0), and Word Length is configured to 9 bits (M1-\/\+M0 = 01), the sent data is handled as a set of u16. In this case, Size must indicate the number of u16 provided through p\+Data. 
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyParams}{Parámetros}
{\em huart} & Pointer to a \doxylink{group___u_a_r_t___exported___types_ga5de4a49eb132735325e706f406c69d6e}{UART\+\_\+\+Handle\+Type\+Def} structure that contains the configuration information for the specified UART module. \\
\hline
{\em p\+Data} & Pointer to data buffer (u8 or u16 data elements). \\
\hline
{\em Size} & Amount of data elements (u8 or u16) to be sent \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

Sends an amount of data in non blocking mode. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
When UART parity is not enabled (PCE = 0), and Word Length is configured to 9 bits (M1-\/\+M0 = 01), the sent data is handled as a set of u16. In this case, Size must indicate the number of u16 provided through p\+Data. 
\end{DoxyNote}

Provide Rx Event type that has lead to Rx\+Event callback execution. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
When \doxylink{group___u_a_r_t___exported___functions___group2_gae70c60d3f42f1c205ebc834de99342a7}{HAL\+\_\+\+UARTEx\+\_\+\+Receive\+To\+Idle\+\_\+\+IT()} or \doxylink{group___u_a_r_t___exported___functions___group2_gaf482a22a09d594e0aa687937aef17949}{HAL\+\_\+\+UARTEx\+\_\+\+Receive\+To\+Idle\+\_\+\+DMA()} API are called, progress of reception process is provided to application through calls of Rx Event callback (either default one \doxylink{group___u_a_r_t___exported___functions___group2_gac44d95ed4163a10ff44353100c81368d}{HAL\+\_\+\+UARTEx\+\_\+\+Rx\+Event\+Callback()} or user registered one). As several types of events could occur (IDLE event, Half Transfer, or Transfer Complete), this function allows to retrieve the Rx Event type that has lead to Rx Event callback execution. 

This function is expected to be called within the user implementation of Rx Event Callback, in order to provide the accurate value : In Interrupt Mode :
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item HAL\+\_\+\+UART\+\_\+\+RXEVENT\+\_\+\+TC : when Reception has been completed (expected nb of data has been received)
\item HAL\+\_\+\+UART\+\_\+\+RXEVENT\+\_\+\+IDLE : when Idle event occurred prior reception has been completed (nb of received data is lower than expected one) In DMA Mode :
\item HAL\+\_\+\+UART\+\_\+\+RXEVENT\+\_\+\+TC : when Reception has been completed (expected nb of data has been received)
\item HAL\+\_\+\+UART\+\_\+\+RXEVENT\+\_\+\+HT : when half of expected nb of data has been received
\item HAL\+\_\+\+UART\+\_\+\+RXEVENT\+\_\+\+IDLE : when Idle event occurred prior reception has been completed (nb of received data is lower than expected one). In DMA mode, Rx\+Event callback could be called several times; When DMA is configured in Normal Mode, HT event does not stop Reception process; When DMA is configured in Circular Mode, HT, TC or IDLE events don\textquotesingle{}t stop Reception process; 
\end{DoxyItemize}
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyParams}{Parámetros}
{\em huart} & UART handle. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Valores devueltos}
{\em Rx} & Event Type (returned value will be a value of \doxylink{group___u_a_r_t___rx_event___type___values}{UART Rx\+Event type values}) \\
\hline
\end{DoxyRetVals}

\doxymbox{\hyperlink{stm32f1xx__hal__def_8h_a63c0679d1cb8b8c684fbb0632743478f}{HAL\+\_\+\+Status\+Type\+Def}} HAL\+\_\+\+UARTEx\+\_\+\+Receive\+To\+Idle (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{group___u_a_r_t___exported___types_ga5de4a49eb132735325e706f406c69d6e}{UART\+\_\+\+Handle\+Type\+Def}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{huart}{, }\item[{uint8\+\_\+t \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{p\+Data}{, }\item[{uint16\+\_\+t}]{Size}{, }\item[{uint16\+\_\+t \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{Rx\+Len}{, }\item[{uint32\+\_\+t}]{Timeout}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



{\ttfamily \+\#include "{}\doxymbox{\hyperlink{stm32f1xx__hal__uart_8h}{stm32f1xx\+\_\+hal\+\_\+uart.\+h}}"{}}



Receive an amount of data in blocking mode till either the expected number of data is received or an IDLE event occurs. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
HAL\+\_\+\+OK is returned if reception is completed (expected number of data has been received) or if reception is stopped after IDLE event (less than the expected number of data has been received) In this case, Rx\+Len output parameter indicates number of data available in reception buffer. 

When UART parity is not enabled (PCE = 0), and Word Length is configured to 9 bits (M = 01), the received data is handled as a set of uint16\+\_\+t. In this case, Size must indicate the number of uint16\+\_\+t available through p\+Data. 
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyParams}{Parámetros}
{\em huart} & UART handle. \\
\hline
{\em p\+Data} & Pointer to data buffer (uint8\+\_\+t or uint16\+\_\+t data elements). \\
\hline
{\em Size} & Amount of data elements (uint8\+\_\+t or uint16\+\_\+t) to be received. \\
\hline
{\em Rx\+Len} & Number of data elements finally received (could be lower than Size, in case reception ends on IDLE event) \\
\hline
{\em Timeout} & Timeout duration expressed in ms (covers the whole reception sequence). \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

{\ttfamily \+\#include "{}\doxymbox{\hyperlink{stm32f1xx__hal__uart_8h}{stm32f1xx\+\_\+hal\+\_\+uart.\+h}}"{}}



Receive an amount of data in DMA mode till either the expected number of data is received or an IDLE event occurs. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
Reception is initiated by this function call. Further progress of reception is achieved thanks to DMA services, transferring automatically received data elements in user reception buffer and calling registered callbacks at half/\+end of reception. UART IDLE events are also used to consider reception phase as ended. In all cases, callback execution will indicate number of received data elements. 

When the UART parity is enabled (PCE = 1), the received data contain the parity bit (MSB position). 

When UART parity is not enabled (PCE = 0), and Word Length is configured to 9 bits (M = 01), the received data is handled as a set of uint16\+\_\+t. In this case, Size must indicate the number of uint16\+\_\+t available through p\+Data. 
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyParams}{Parámetros}
{\em huart} & UART handle. \\
\hline
{\em p\+Data} & Pointer to data buffer (uint8\+\_\+t or uint16\+\_\+t data elements). \\
\hline
{\em Size} & Amount of data elements (uint8\+\_\+t or uint16\+\_\+t) to be received. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Valores devueltos}
{\em \doxylink{group___h_a_l}{HAL}} & status \\
\hline
\end{DoxyRetVals}

Receive an amount of data in interrupt mode till either the expected number of data is received or an IDLE event occurs. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Nota}
Reception is initiated by this function call. Further progress of reception is achieved thanks to UART interrupts raised by RXNE and IDLE events. Callback is called at end of reception indicating number of received data elements. 

When UART parity is not enabled (PCE = 0), and Word Length is configured to 9 bits (M = 01), the received data is handled as a set of uint16\+\_\+t. In this case, Size must indicate the number of uint16\+\_\+t available through p\+Data. 
\end{DoxyNote}

{\em huart} & UART handle. \\
\hline
{\em p\+Data} & Pointer to data buffer (uint8\+\_\+t or uint16\+\_\+t data elements). \\
\hline
{\em Size} & Amount of data elements (uint8\+\_\+t or uint16\+\_\+t) to be received. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
